CATALOGUE 2025

## Pagina 2

**Making riding affordable without compromising on quality, was and is the mission of ProX Racing Parts. Founded in 1980 by passionate motorcycle people, today known as the worldwide leader in two wheel and four wheel OEM replacement parts and more. And we are still developing...**

**Our strong connection at all levels of racing will ensure our passion and commitment of winning and having fun is in our products. We are proud of our heritage and are excited for the future. We thank you for being with us. If you are new to the team, we look forward to working with you. It is all about having fun and winning races. That makes us happy!**

## Pagina 4

ENGINE product overview

~~<u>All kits include the piston, pin, rings and clips</u>~~

~~<u>Available in standard graded sizes (A, B, C, +) & Bore oversizes</u>~~

~~<u>Coated for ultimate performance Compatible for nikasil plated and sleeved cylinders</u>~~

# PISTONS

PISTON KITS  •  PISTON RINGS  •  PISTON PINS  •  NEEDLE BEARINGS  •  PISTON CLIPS

The heart of our business! Our ProX pistons are mainly manufactured in Japan at Japanese O.E. manufacturers. The pistons are precision machined on the latest, state of the art CNC equipment. Our pistons are made of the same low-expansion and low-friction alloys as used by all major (Japanese) piston manufacturers.

Coated for ultimate performance

Most of the ProX pistons are skirt or dome coated. Our OEM style tin coated pistons assist the wear in process when you just started the engine. We have developed a new skirt coating that not only assists the wear in process, but also helps reducing friction after the wear in.

ProX piston kits are a genuine, first class solution to replace the original piston of your motorcycle, ATV, personal watercraft or snowmobile engine.

This black coating is made from a delicate mix of materials and will increase performance.

- 2 - PRO-X.COM

## Pagina 5

Manufactured by leading Japanese
OEM factories
Double forged on dedicated forgings
Shot peened & barreled
Single rod kit available where OEM
only offers a complete crankshaft
CONNECTING RODS

# CONNECTING RODS  •  BEARINGS  •  WASHERS  •  PINS

One of our core product lines, where we became famous with as well. Our ProX connecting rod kits, including the bearing, pin and washers are made out of the toughest alloys available today and are made in Japan! OEM specifications and tolerances are meticulously maintained, a guarantee for easy installation and troucle free performance.

## Shot peening & barreling

The design of a connecting rod is very important to guarantee the lifespan and the performance. Our connecting rods are barreled and shot peened to improve the surface texture. Shot peening eliminates residual stress and smoothly blends the forging surfaces.

Double forged on dedicated forgings

This process maintains superior strength, durability and dimensional consistency through improved grain flow.

## ProX oiling system

Our ProX oiling slots are precisely located to maximize lower rod oiling and extend bearing life. Even under the most extreme conditions the rod will do its job. ProX connecting rod kits come complete with crankpin, big-end bearing, small-end bearing and washers.

# CLUTCH COMPONENTS

## CLUTCH BASKETS  •  INNER HUBS  •  PRESSURE PLATES  •  SPRING KITS

•  CLUTCH RUBBERS  •  FRICTION & STEEL PLATES

ProX clutch components are made by leading Japanese O.E. manufacturers from the finest materials. The high quality and well-priced clutch components are chosen by many riders and race teams.

We stock clutch parts for almost every Dirt Bike and ATV applications. ProX offers the complete package to guarantee the best performance!

- 4 - PRO-X.COM

## Pagina 7

A direct and cost-eff i cient
OEM replacement
Manufactured of premium grade steel
and titanium
Incremental shim sizes by 0.025 mm
Titanium to valve steel conversion kits
increase durability

VALVE TRAIN COMPONENTS

CAM CHAINS  •  VALVE SEALS  •  VALVE SHIMS VALVE REPLACEMENT KITS

# •  STEEL VALVES TITANIUM VALVES

When 4-stroke engines became more and more common in most of the powersports applications, ProX has developed a wide program of valve train components. Our wide range of steel and titanium engine valves are top of the bill!

Next to our selection of single valves ProX offers a wide range of conversion kits to replace the more expensive and less durable titanium valves into steel valves kits including the matching valve spring.

When repacing valves, we recommend to replace the cam chain as well. And guess what, ProX offers a wide range of cam chains for many Dirt Bikes and ATV’s!

After installing valves, the clearance need to be matched to OEM specifications. To do this job right we offer many sizes of shims in many thicknesses. Besides incremental sizes of 0.05 mm, we also offer all “in between” sizes 0.025 mm and 0.075 mm. Together with our valve stem seals, you will be all set to replace the valves.

## Pagina 8

# ENGINE product overview

~~<u>Manufactured of premium base materials Precision cut for optimal f</u>~~ i ~~<u>t and perfect seal</u>~~

~~<u>Cost eff</u>~~ i ~~<u>cient assembly</u>~~

## GASKETS

COMPLETE SETS  •  TOP END SETS  •  HEAD & BASE SETS

- WATERPUMP REBUILD SETS  •  CLUTCH COVER  •  IGNITION COVER

Prox gaskets are manufactured by high quality factories to ProX specifications to ensure top quality sealing. Precision engineering and the use of first class materials guarantee the quality you demand from a gasket. The gaskets are complementary to our pistons, crankshafts and connecting rods, so we developed a range of different sets, which will suit your rebuilding job best.

To ensure superior performance, we manufacture our gaskets from a wide range of materials. Whether the situation calls for the latest in advanced technology like our multi-layer steel gasket or a synthetic blend fiber, ProX offers a wide range of products your application will suit best.

## BEARINGS

~~<u>High quality, mainly Japanese bearings</u>~~

~~<u>Developed to OEM specif</u>~~ i ~~<u>cations</u>~~

~~<u>A direct and cost eff</u>~~ i ~~<u>cient OEM replacement</u>~~

~~<u>Available in different clearance ratings</u>~~

A-ARM BEARING KITS  •  BALL BEARINGS  •  STEERING BEARINGS

- WHEEL BEARINGS  •  WHEEL SPACER KITS

Many bearings for many applications and purposes are available from ProX Racing Parts. A wide variety of high quality ball and roller bearings, like crankshaft bearings, steering bearings, swingarm bearings or wheel bearings, ProX has them all. And of course in the best quality available.

You will only find top quality brands from Japan like KOYO, NTN, HIC, MBS, etc. This will enable you to rebuild your specific assembly to, reliable and as new specifications.

- 6 - PRO-X.COM

## Pagina 9

SERVICE product overview

All components you need in one kit
NOK Front fork seals and dust caps

SUSPENSION

FRONT FORK SEALS  •  DUST CAPS  •  BUSHING KITS  •  SHOCK BEARING KITS

- REAR SHOCK SEAL HEAD KITS  •  SWINGARM BEARING KITS  •  LINKAGE BEARING KITS

To rebuild your front fork, swing arm or linkage, ProX offers a wide program of front fork seals, dust caps, swing arm bearing kits, swing arm linkage bearing kits, upper shock bearing kits and lower shock bearing kits for many Dirt Bike and ATV applications.

All kits come as complete set with bearings, bushings and seals. All these kits are pre-packed and are ready for easy installation. ProX front fork oil seals and dust caps are made at the O.E. manufacturers in Japan. For almost every Dirt Bike application we have the right seal. The high quality and competitive pricing made them a very popular item in our range. The way to go!

Front and rear brake pads
Self cleaning disc rotors for
ultimate performance

BRAKE PARTS

BRAKE DISCS  •  BRAKE PADS  •  BRAKE REBUILD KITS

To stop all the horse power our parts give your engine, we also offer you our brake parts. Our brake pads, brake discs and rebuild kits for master cylinder brake pumps or calipers offer the best performance! For the perfect match we recommend using ProX brake disks with a matching pad compound. The caliper rebuild kits and master cylinder rebuild kits are assembled with high quality components all designed and manufactured to meet or exceed the OEM parts. Each kit contains all the necessary components to rebuild.

## Pagina 10

product overview

# SERVICE

~~<u>Aluminium and steel rear sprockets</u>~~

~~<u>Drive chain in standard and gold</u>~~

~~<u>Mud grooved front sprockets</u>~~

~~<u>Chain rollers and more…</u>~~

## TRANSMISSION PARTS

CHAIN ROLLER  •  COUNTERSHAFT SEAL KITS  •  DRIVE CHAIN

- FRONT SPROCKETS  •  REAR SPROCKETS

Get the power to your wheels! ProX offers sprockets and drive chains for your secondary transmission! A complete line of front and rear sprockets and complementary parts like drive chains, countershaft seal kits, chain rollers and sprocket bolts is available.

We offer aluminium and steel rear sprockets and our drive chains are available in a standard and a gold version. The perfect match!

~~<u>Black PVC outer jacket for maximum f</u>~~ l ~~<u>exibility and durability</u>~~

~~<u>Steel inner casing, tightlycoiled for low compression</u>~~

~~<u>A pre-lubricated nylon inner sleeve guarantees smooth operation and long life</u>~~

~~<u>Steel inner casing that is tightly coiled for low compression</u>~~

## CABLES

CLUTCH CABLES  •  THROTTLE CABLES

Our clutch & throttle cables are equipped with pre-lubricated nylon inner sleeves and tightly coiled inner casings. Combined with a flexible PVC outer jacket and strong steel inner cable they offer high durability and smooth control.

The cables are easy to assemble due to the appropriate length for your application and come with all necessary mounting parts. Our thorough quality control and use of high quality materials guarantee the perfect fitment. ProX Racing Parts offers a wide range of control cables for an extended range of Dirt Bike applications.

- 8 - PRO-X.COM

ProX Carburetor rebuild kits are

SERVICE product overview

FUEL MANAGEMENT

# CARBURETOR REBUILD KITS  •  CARBURETOR JETS

Fuel systems are becoming more and more digital by having injection systems, but a lot of Dirt Bikes, ATV’s  and many other application do have regular carburetors, where main and pilot jets are used.

ProX Racing Parts offers a wide program of single jets and complete carburetor rebuild kits.

High quality f i lters manufactured from
the f i nest materials
Optimized to secure maximum f l ow

FILTERS

# OIL FILTERS  •  AIR FILTERS

ProX Racing Parts oil & air filters are high quality filters manufactured from the finest materials only. To reach the high level of quality and consistency, the raw materials are supplied by a selected group of leading manufacturers in the industry. The various quality control steps in the manufacturing process makes the ProX oil and air filters one of the best filters in the world. Available for a wide range of Dirtbike and Sport ATV model.

Our ProX Air Filters are made of premium dual stage bonded foam to filter out the smallest dirt particles and at the same time offers optimal airflow. A thick, flat foam sealing ring provides a perfect seat against the air box, protecting against dirt and sand even in the most extreme riding conditions. The exclusive design and special production process brings the perfect combination of protection and performance!
